### cinematic film scene #82 — Scene 2 - The fantasy flight on the TV

- Source: https://higgsfield.ai/blog/seedance4k-breakdown ([notes](seedance4k-breakdown.md))
- Model / settings: Assets: GPT Image 2 (4K sheets), Nano Banana Pro (props), Soul Cinema (locations/characters); video: Seedance 2.0 4K; prompts written by a Claude "prompt-writer" skill

~~~~text
SCENE CONTEXT @woman flies on @fantasy-dragon, a large flying creature, through a colossal jungle of mile-high fantastical trees. Wordless throughout, she steers with her body and pure joy — laughing and whooping as @fantasy-dragon pulls off daring tricks: a sheer near-vertical plunge down an immensely tall 90° waterfall almost grazing the water, a low skim across the pool, passing through a cloud of giant butterflies with a single breathtaking slow-motion moment, gliding between branches and lianas past leaping monkeys, threading a razor-narrow gap, then climbing straight up the trunks and bursting above the canopy into a vast reveal of the whole world. One continuous soaring flight.ACTIVE REFERENCES @fantasy-dragon: the flying creature. 100% matches the reference, identical design every frame. @woman: the rider, seated just behind @fantasy-dragon's shoulders, hands on its shoulder-ridge, leaning, ducking and bracing into every bank, dive and roll. 100% matches the reference, same person throughout.CREATURE SCALE & WING PHYSICS (behavior only, design from reference) @fantasy-dragon is large — wingspan of a small aircraft, body big enough to carry @woman easily, powerful and substantial. Its wings obey real flight physics with three clear modes: (1) in the vertical dive, wings tuck in tight and folded against the body, streamlined for a fast nose-down plunge; (2) when gliding and weaving through the trees, wings hold wide, spread and still, soaring on locked wings — steering by tilting and angling the spread wings and body, not by flapping; (3) when climbing and gaining height, broad, deliberate, weighty wingbeats with a clear up- and down-stroke through a large arc, the membrane flexing and the whole body rising and dipping with each beat. Mass and inertia read in every motion.FORMAT MODE Single continuous take with no cuts. One in-camera speed ramp: real-time motion throughout, ramping down smoothly into super slow motion for a single butterfly close-up around the 4–6s mark, then snapping back hard and instantly to full real-time speed. Real-time everywhere else. 15 seconds, 16:9 anamorphic widescreen. The clip is purely visual and sound-effect driven, with a fully music-free, speech-free, caption-free track — natural ambience and the rider's wordless voice only.CAMERA POSITION LOCK — ALWAYS BEHIND The camera stays locked behind @fantasy-dragon and @woman for the entire clip — a chase camera that follows directly from the rear, holding their backs in frame with the world rushing toward and past them. We always see them from behind: the back of @woman's head and shoulders, @fantasy-dragon's wings filling the foreground, the jungle opening up ahead. During the slow-motion beat the camera keeps its rear position and simply racks focus onto a butterfly hovering close to the lens, with @fantasy-dragon and @woman gliding softly just beyond it. The camera rides their tail through the dive, skim, glide, gap and climb, keeping the same rear vantage throughout.THE LOCATION (full description — maximum color and life) A surreal, monumental rainforest of impossible scale. The trees are mile-high fantastical giants — colossal pillar-trunks wider than towers, bark in deep browns and mossy greens, crowns vanishing into golden mist; upper branches fuse into vaulted green cathedral arches. Thick horizontal branches and long hanging lianas cross the flight path at many depths, and one pair of trunks leans together to form a razor-narrow vertical gap. A colossal, immensely tall waterfall drops in a sheer, near-vertical 90° wall — a vast curtain of falling water plunging straight down an enormous cliff-trunk from high in the canopy, falling and falling all the way to the forest floor, into a glowing turquoise pool and mist-filled gorge, sunlight throwing rainbows across the spray. The whole jungle blazes with color and life: cascading flowers in magenta, violet, orange and gold, glowing bioluminescent blooms, hanging curtains of pink and blue blossom, giant lily-pads and lotus on the pool, emerald spiral ferns, turquoise moss, dangling vines heavy with fruit, oversized jewel-toned leaves, drifting seed-pods and floating spores catching the light. It teems with diverse wildlife at every depth — dense flocks of brilliant birds (scarlet macaw-like fliers, cobalt songbirds, gold-and-green parrots, iridescent hummingbirds, big slow herons over the water), vast drifting swarms of giant luminous butterflies in sapphire, amber and emerald, dragonflies and glowing fireflies, troops of long-limbed monkeys swinging and leaping from tree to tree, neon tree-frogs and chameleons on the trunks, a coiled serpent on a branch, a pair of huge gentle deer-like grazers and a family of small spotted creatures drinking at the pool far below. High screen-right, warm golden sun rakes down through the canopy in thick volumetric god-rays, catching waterfall-mist, pollen and floating spores. Everything alive is tiny against the trees. Deep parallax: foreground vines, flowers and leaves rush past, midground trunks and wildlife slide by, the far canopy and sky shift slowly behind.FIRST FRAME AND SPATIAL BLOCKING The first visible frame already contains @fantasy-dragon and @woman, seen from behind, hanging high at the very top of the immensely tall 90° waterfall — tiny against the towering wall of water, already tipping nose-down into the vertical plunge with wings folding in. The camera sits directly behind them. @fantasy-dragon center frame, x 50%, y 40%, wings tucking tight, pointing straight down the falling water; @woman just ahead of the lens on its back, leaning low and forward into the dive, the back of her head and streaming hair in frame, laughing. The flight path drops straight down the huge waterfall almost touching its surface, flares open just above the pool, skims the water, rises into the butterflies and the slow-motion beat, glides through the trees, threads the narrow gap, then climbs upward — always viewed from the rear.OPTICS 107° diagonal field of view, wide rectilinear lens character, camera held very close — 0.5–0.8 m behind @fantasy-dragon's back and wings, almost riding with them. Immediate foreground (@fantasy-dragon's wings and body, @woman's streaming hair, rushing spray, vines and butterflies) looms large; the surrounding jungle spreads wide to all frame edges; straight trunk lines stay straight and rectilinear edge to edge, with subtle chromatic aberration near the corners. During the super-slow-motion beat, the same wide lens racks focus to a single giant butterfly hovering right at the lens, rendering it in crisp, detailed close-up while @fantasy-dragon and @woman soften slightly just beyond — no lens cut, a smooth focus pull. Immersive, body-near, action-cam-mounted-behind-the-rider intimacy with huge environmental width.ACTION TIMING 0.0s–3.0s — THE TALL PLUNGE & WATER SKIM: from directly behind, @fantasy-dragon tips over the very top of the immensely tall 90° waterfall and drops in a long near-vertical nose-down plunge, wings tucked tight and folded, streamlined and accelerating straight down the towering wall of falling water — flying down so close it almost grazes the rushing surface the whole way, spray and rainbow light streaking past. Near the forest floor it flares its wings wide open and arcs out of the dive, leveling inches above the glowing turquoise pool, wingtips and tail-fan slicing twin lines of spray, herons scattering ahead. @woman trails her fingers through the water, shrieking with joy. 3.0s–4.0s — INTO THE SWARM: @fantasy-dragon rises off the water with a few powerful wingbeats toward a vast cloud of giant luminous butterflies; as they approach, the swarm bursts apart and scatters in every direction — sapphire, amber and emerald wings whirling outward around them. @woman laughs, arms wide. 4.0s–6.0s — SUPER SLOW-MOTION BUTTERFLY (no cut): as they pass through the swarm, one large butterfly drifts up right to the lens and time ramps smoothly down into super slow motion, holding for about two seconds on a beautiful close-up of this single butterfly — its big iridescent wings beating slowly and gracefully, every detail visible: glowing sapphire-amber-emerald scales, fine vein patterns, light shining through the translucent membrane, tiny shimmering edges — while @fantasy-dragon and @woman glide on softly just behind it. Then, sharply and instantly, time snaps back to full real-time speed — a hard, sudden return to motion as the butterfly whips away and the flight surges forward again. 6.0s–9.0s — THE GLIDING WEAVE PAST THE MONKEYS: back at full speed, @fantasy-dragon spreads its wings wide and still and soars on locked wings, gliding silently between two massive crossing branches and a curtain of hanging lianas, banking and tilting on its spread wings — no flapping — leaves, fruit and flowers whipping past. Troops of long-limbed monkeys leap from tree to tree across the path and cling to the trunks, heads snapping round to stare after @fantasy-dragon and @woman in wide-eyed surprise as they glide by; parrots and hummingbirds scatter. @woman ducks low and lets out a delighted whoop. 9.0s–11.5s — THE NARROW GAP: still gliding, @fantasy-dragon folds its wings and shoots through a razor-narrow vertical gap between two leaning trunks — bark walls blurring past inches from the lens — then snaps its wings open on the far side in a corkscrew roll past a swooping flock. @woman throws her head back, laughing and cheering with pure exhilaration. 11.5s–15.0s — THE CLIMB AND REVEAL: @fantasy-dragon pitches up and powers straight upward along the colossal trunks with broad, heaving wingbeats, bark, branches, flowers and birds streaking downward past the lens as the camera (still behind) tilts up and cranes back. They burst up through the top of the canopy into open golden sky. The camera pulls back into a colossal wide establishing shot revealing the WHOLE world at once — an endless sea of mile-high trees rolling to the horizon, rivers of mist and the distant waterfall gorge between them, flocks like drifting confetti, @fantasy-dragon and @woman a bright speck rising above it all. @woman laughs breathlessly in wonder as @fantasy-dragon levels off and glances back at her with a soft chirp. Hold on the awe.PHYSICS @fantasy-dragon moves with the three wing modes above — wings tucked and streamlined in the long vertical plunge, wide and locked while gliding through the trees, broad and heaving while climbing. The flare out of the dive shows real aerodynamic load as the open wings catch the air and bend the fall into a level arc. In the slow-motion beat the butterfly's wingbeat reads in exquisite slow detail — wings flexing, bending and rippling through the stroke, scales catching light, body bobbing gently. Mass and inertia carry through every bank, roll, glide and level-out. Down the waterfall and over the water, wingtips and tail cut real spray trails and leave a wake; mist beads in the air. @woman's body shifts, ducks and braces with every move, weight transferring; her hair and clothes stream and snap with airspeed, gusts and spray. Branches, lianas, fruit, flowers, leaves and butterfly wings bend, scatter and recoil as they pass. Birds, butterflies, monkeys and ground animals react with real evasive and curious motion — monkeys leaping between trees and turning to look. Every motion carries weight and momentum with clear acceleration and deceleration.LIGHTING Primary light is warm golden sun high on screen-right, raking through the canopy in volumetric god-rays and catching mist, pollen, spores and butterfly wings. Maximum vibrancy and luminosity — deep emerald and jade greens, cobalt-to-turquoise sky and pool, warm gold accents, blazing pops of scarlet, magenta, violet, sapphire and coral from flowers, butterflies, birds and @fantasy-dragon. During the slow-motion close-up, sunlight glows through the butterfly's translucent wings, lighting up the iridescent color. At the waterfall, sun through the spray throws a vivid rainbow and bright speculars off the water. In the narrow gap the light goes briefly cool and shadowed, then bursts back into brilliant daylight on the far side. As they break above the canopy the light opens into bright open daylight across the whole landscape. Rich, saturated, glowing and painterly with deep, clean color. Sun rim-lights @fantasy-dragon's wings and @woman's hair; soft environmental bounce fills the shadows.AUDIO SFX only: the roar and hiss of the towering waterfall swelling louder as they plunge down it, a whoosh of rushing air in the long vertical dive, a soft thump of air as the wings flare open, spattering spray over the water, then soft fluttering as the swarm bursts apart. As time ramps into super slow motion the sound stretches low and dreamy — a deep, slowed flutter and a soft hush — then cuts back instantly with a sharp snap to real-time as full speed returns: a sudden surge of rushing wind, then the quiet rushing wind of the silent glide, chittering monkey calls as they leap and react, a sharp whoosh and echo through the narrow gap, broad steady wing-beats building on the final climb, @fantasy-dragon's soft friendly chirps (especially as it glances back), and @woman's delighted breath, laughter, gasps and joyful whoops throughout — wordless. Around them: rich birdsong, insect hum, rushing leaves and water. @fantasy-dragon's only vocal sound is its gentle chirp. The track stays fully natural and music-free from start to finish.POSITIVE LOCKS Colossal surreal scale held throughout — @fantasy-dragon and @woman are a tiny bright speck against mile-high trees and the towering waterfall, and the final wide reveals the entire landscape at once. The whole clip is one continuous take with no cuts: a smooth ramp down into the super-slow-motion butterfly close-up, then a sharp, instant snap back to full real-time speed. The camera stays behind them the whole time, holding their backs in frame, racking focus to the butterfly only for the slow beat. @fantasy-dragon is large with physically believable wings: tucked tight in the long 90° plunge almost grazing the water, opening near the ground, gliding wide and still between the trees, and beating broad and powerful only on the climb; it keeps the exact reference design every frame, and @woman is the same person throughout, matching the reference. Coherent, solid jungle and tree architecture with true perspective through the tall vertical waterfall, water skim, butterfly slow-motion, gliding weave and the narrow gap; vibrant glowing color and dense, varied wildlife — birds, butterflies, leaping curious monkeys, ground animals — all the way through; charming, physically believable flight with natural, film-like organic texture, lifelike surfaces, soft natural eyes, and a clean unmarked frame.
~~~~

~~~~text
SCENE CONTEXT Evening, same living room. @ADIL sits on the sofa, fully fired-up by something on the TV we never see. From the unseen kitchen his mother calls him to come eat; too hyped to break away, he mumbles a delayed reply, grabs the remote off the low table, flips to another channel and keeps watching — he does not get up.ACTIVE REFERENCES @ADIL — young man, bright-eyed and hyped, immersed in the off-frame screen. 100% matches the reference. @REMOTE — ordinary household TV remote, resting on the low table in front of him at the start. 100% matches the reference. (Mother is an offscreen English voice only — no in-frame reference, never enters the shot.)LOCATION MAP @ADIL'S-ROOM — STYLE REFERENCE ONLY, not a fixed keyframe; model extends the world, never pinned 1:1. Foreground: the low coffee table with @REMOTE on it. Midground: the sofa, @ADIL facing forward toward the off-frame TV. Background: the room and a doorway/hallway leading off frame-right to the unseen kitchen; a side window frame-left as the fading light source. Lived-in room, faint haze in the air.FIRST FRAME / BLOCKING Non-empty opening frame: @ADIL already leaning forward off the sofa back, elbows on knees, eyes bright and locked on the off-frame screen, body bouncing slightly with excitement; @REMOTE sitting on the table in front of him. The off-frame TV sits directly ahead of the sofa, its glow on his face — never shown in frame. Asymmetric framing, subject already in motion.FORMAT MODE Timed multishot, explicit HARD CUTs at the stated seconds; cuts only at those points, the camera does not cut on its own.OPTICS Fixed shooting distance per shot — no zoom, no push-in; crop size changes only on the cut. SHOT 1 MS at 47°, SHOT 2 CU at 29°, SHOT 3 MS on his hands at 47°. No drift mid-segment.CAMERA Handheld throughout, live operator breathing, pronounced organic shake and micro-drift, a subtle dutch that resettles — never on sticks, no locked-off framing. Slight low angle in every shot, lens a touch below eye-level looking gently up at him. SHOT 1: slight low-angle handheld medium with restless sway riding his excited energy — feel his hyped momentum. SHOT 2: slight low-angle handheld close, jittery micro-drift holding on his grinning face through the delay — sit in the beat before he answers. SHOT 3: slight low-angle handheld on his hands as he grabs @REMOTE off the table and clicks, shaky drift as the glow shifts — he changes the channel and stays put.ACTION SHOT 1 (0:00–0:06) — @ADIL leans forward, eyes locked on the off-frame screen, a grin tugging, body bouncing slightly, screen glow dancing on his lit-up face. Offscreen his mother calls him to eat; he's too into it — no real reaction, maybe a tiny absent "mm". HARD CUT. SHOT 2 (0:06–0:11) — close on his face, still glued and grinning, light shifting across him; the offscreen voice calls again, sharper. A beat — his eyes flicker, he huffs a laugh, mumbles a reply without looking away. HARD CUT. SHOT 3 (0:11–0:15) — still locked on the screen, he reaches forward, picks @REMOTE up off the table, points it at the screen, thumb presses — soft click — the off-frame glow shifts hue as the channel changes; he settles back a touch, eyes never leaving the screen, still watching, not getting up. HARD CUT.PERFORMANCE Pure excitement and immersion, only half-acknowledging the call — genuine and human, never cartoonish. Bright engaged eyes with screen catch-lights, a grin tugging, small body bounce; on the second call a flicker in the eyes and a huffed laugh before the mumbled reply. Pore-level realism — vellus hair, capillary flush, visible breath and chest rise.PHYSICS @REMOTE carries real weight as he lifts it from the table. The cushion holds his lean. Correct contact shadows on the sofa and table. Nothing floats.LIGHTING Natural light only. Window key from frame-left, going dimmer and warmer toward evening, camera on the shadow side, faint atmospheric haze. The off-frame TV glow is the moving light on his face — cool, shifting hue on the channel change in SHOT 3. WB locked 5200K.COLOR GRADE Warm fading-daylight room neutrals as the dominant material, cool TV screen glow on his face as the secondary beam, with a chartreuse #d1ef17 accent living only in the @REMOTE standby LED, a flicker of the screen glow and a glint on a lens flare — color tied to source and surface, not a flat list.AUDIO NO MUSIC. SFX ONLY — diegetic sound and live audio throughout. No score, no soundtrack, no background music of any kind. Room ambience, the soft compression of the cushion, a soft remote click, the low hum of the off-frame TV. Dialogue (English, audio only, no subtitles): Mother (offscreen): "Honey, dinner's ready! Come eat!" Mother (offscreen, sharper): "Did you hear me? Come on, it's getting cold!" @ADIL (mumbled, eyes still on screen): "Yeah— one sec, Mom!"STYLE 8K photorealistic — no 3D render, no game engine, no game-cutscene aesthetic. Naturalistic master cinematography, fine film grain.OUTPUT SETTINGS 16:9. Real-time throughout, no slow-motion.POSITIVE LOCKS Handheld shaky operator-feel in every shot, slight low angle throughout, fixed distance per shot with crop changing only on the cut — no zoom, no push-in. @ADIL stays seated, changes the channel and keeps watching — he does not stand up and does not turn the TV off. The off-frame TV is never in frame, not even blurred — felt only through the glow on his face. The mother stays offscreen, an English voice only. He picks @REMOTE up off the table before clicking; @REMOTE stays normal household remote size. Screen content stays unseen — no logos, no UI, no subtitles. Contact shadows on sofa and table read clearly. Wardrobe comes from the @ADIL reference and stays identical across all three cuts. Eyes stay natural — no eye glow.
~~~~

~~~~text
SCENE CONTEXT A vast snowy mountain range in cold daylight. From one fixed vantage a super-telephoto lens zooms from a wide view of the peaks down to a snow leopard threading its way down the rocky ledges, settling on a half-body framing as breath steams from its nose. One continuous shot, 10 seconds. The leopard is on a distant slope across miles of air from the camera.ACTIVE REFERENCES @mountain — the location (source of truth): a vast range of steep snow-covered peaks, rocky ridgelines and ledges, layered summits fading into cold blue atmospheric haze, cold daylight. 100% matches the reference.THE SNOW LEOPARD (full description, identical every frame) A real snow leopard with thick pale grey-and-white fur dense with black rosettes and spots, pale-green eyes, a broad furry face, broad furry paws, and a very long thick spotted tail. Heavy plush winter coat, stocky muscular build. Keep its exact markings, colors and proportions consistent in every frame — no morphing, no drift.LOCATION MAP Foreground / midground: the steep rocky ledges and snowy steps the leopard descends. Background: layered snow-covered summits fading into cold blue haze. The camera is anchored far away on a single fixed vantage, looking across miles of distance toward the slope.FIRST FRAME / BLOCKING The camera opens at the wide 84° view, the mountains filling the frame and the snow leopard already a tiny speck moving on a distant slope — already descending, nothing frozen. Subject low and to one side of frame, the layered peaks reading top to bottom.FORMAT MODE One continuous shot (oner), 16:9, real-time, no cuts. The camera holds one fixed spot the whole time; only the lens focal length changes — pure optical zoom, the camera does not cut on its own.OPTICS A single massive optical zoom from WIDE 84° FOV to super-telephoto 8° FOV, stopping at a HALF-BODY framing of the leopard (head, chest and shoulders, roughly half the body). Extreme compression at the long end — mountain layers flattened into stacked planes. Genuine long-distance super-tele character: from the moment the zoom pushes long, and strongly by the close end, the image swims and ripples with heat-haze and atmospheric distortion, edges shimmering as if seen through rising air over miles; a constant nervous telephoto micro-tremor, the framing trembling and drifting; the picture grows softer and slightly mushy, fine sharpness falling off into gentle blur and faint digital-zoom smear with light grain; a brief focus hunt before it settles on the cat. The wide start is clean and crisp; these artifacts build hard as it zooms in. Continuous push, no drift between beats.CAMERA Fully static locked-off tripod, anchored on the shadow-and-distance side of the slope, operator axis fixed across the valley. The camera never repositions, dollies or pans — it stays in ONE spot; the only movement is the lens zooming long. 180° shutter motion blur. Wide tonal latitude holding both bright snow highlights and shadowed rock without clipping, gentle highlight roll-off on the lit snow.ACTION Throughout, the snow leopard descends the mountain — picking its way down the rocky ledges and snowy steps at a slow, careful walking pace, sure-footed and deliberate, body low, the long tail counterbalancing, placing each broad paw ledge to ledge. Small clumps and flecks of snow cling to its coat, some shaking loose as it moves. Camera and subject motion stated separately on the 10-second clock: 0.0s–2.5s — camera at the wide 84°, the leopard a tiny speck descending a distant slope. 2.5s–7.5s — a continuous optical zoom-in from 84° toward 8° (camera staying put), the heat-shimmer, telephoto jitter and softness all intensifying as the lens pushes long; the leopard keeps descending. 7.5s–10.0s — the zoom settles and holds at a HALF-BODY framing, the image trembling, soft and shimmering; the leopard pauses, scanning, warm breath vapor puffing and steaming from its nose and mouth with each exhale, curling away in the cold. PERFORMANCE Real big-cat behavior — careful, deliberate downward steps, body low and sure-footed, long tail swaying to counterbalance, broad paws set ledge to ledge, pausing to scan with breath fogging, fully alive from frame one. Pore-level fur realism: thick pale grey-and-white coat dense with black rosettes, snow flecks caught in the fur of shoulders, legs and paws, warm breath steaming from nose and mouth, living pale-green eyes with cold catch-lights.PHYSICS Gravity and inertia respected — balanced downward steps, paws gripping rock and snow, loose snow sliding underfoot, the long tail swaying for balance. Warm breath condenses into visible vapor in the cold air. Snow flecks on the fur jostle and drop with movement.LIGHTING Cold natural daylight, white balance locked at 5600K daylight with a cold cast. Blue atmospheric haze layering the peaks, soft snow highlights, the leopard's pale fur blending into rock and snow, warm breath vapor catching the cold light. Crisp at the wide start; hazier and softer when zoomed long. Atmospheric haze builds across the shot — roughly 20% density at the wide start rising to about 70% at the long compressed end, visible deep into the layered peaks.COLOR GRADE 60:30:10 — dominant cold snow-and-rock neutrals; secondary cold blue atmospheric haze blanketing the layered peaks; accent the warm steam of the leopard's breath catching the light and its pale-green eyes. Cold neutrals as material under flat daylight, the blue as the air itself thickening with distance, the warm breath as the single live spark of heat.AUDIO NO MUSIC. SFX ONLY — diegetic sound and live audio throughout. No score, no soundtrack, no background music of any kind. High-altitude wind, the faint slide of snow and loose rock under the paws, soft audible breathing at the close framing, distant mountain ambience, the muffled quiet of altitude.STYLE 8K, photorealistic, live-action long-distance super-telephoto wildlife capture — real footage, not a render or game engine. Naturalistic cinematography, one continuous locked-off zoom, fine natural grain building with the long lens.OUTPUT SETTINGS 8K, 16:9, 24fps, real-time throughout, total duration 10 seconds, one continuous shot.POSITIVE LOCKS The camera stays in one fixed spot — static tripod, pure optical zoom only, never cuts and never leaves its spot. The snow leopard keeps the exact same fur, markings, pale-green eyes and proportions throughout — no identity drift; the location stays exactly @mountain, same mountain range throughout. One continuous optical zoom from 84° wide to 8° super-tele, stopping at a HALF-BODY framing. The long-lens look stays clearly realistic and imperfect — visible heat-haze swim, telephoto jitter, soft mushy grain, all building as it zooms in. The leopard descends the rocky ledges sure-footed, with visible breath vapor and snow flecks in its fur. 10 seconds, real-time.
~~~~

### cinematic film scene #86 — Scene 5 - The truck vs the snow monster

- Source: https://higgsfield.ai/blog/seedance4k-breakdown ([notes](seedance4k-breakdown.md))
- Model / settings: Assets: GPT Image 2 (4K sheets), Nano Banana Pro (props), Soul Cinema (locations/characters); video: Seedance 2.0 4K; prompts written by a Claude "prompt-writer" skill

~~~~text
SCENE CONTEXT In a frozen post-apocalyptic wasteland, @truck1 — an armored snow-truck — races down a white highway at full power, weaving through a frozen graveyard of wrecks and ice barricades while desperately trying to outrun a massive blizzard-avalanche that chases it from directly behind. The man is the driver, both hands on the steering wheel; the woman sits beside him in the passenger seat and does not touch the wheel. @monst, a colossal ice monster, emerges out of the churning blizzard behind the truck, dives beneath the snow like a shark and surfaces still behind it, lunges and just misses with its claws, then heaves a massive hand under the truck and flips it. The truck tumbles, lands and rolls toward a low ground-level camera, coming to rest upside down right in front of the lens. Grounded, photoreal, high-budget fantasy realism — every surface, particle, and performance reads as captured live, not generated. ACTIVE REFERENCES @truck1: the heavy armored Mad-Max-style snow-truck — welded steel cab with riveted armor plating, roof rack of amber running lamps and whip antenna, steel snowplow blade at the nose, oversized deep-lug studded snow tires up front, studded rubber snow-tracks at the rear, exhaust stacks, covered cargo box. Weathered, ice-crusted, steel-grey. 100% matches the reference. @monst: the towering humanoid blizzard monster of compacted churning snow and translucent ice — frost-crystal surfaces, jagged icicle growths, pale softly glowing almond eyes, a maw of icicle teeth, massive clawed hands of fused ice shards. Edges half-dissolved into drifting snow, never fully solid; it forms from and emerges out of the blizzard, can submerge beneath the snow and resurface, and relentlessly chases the truck from behind. 100% matches the reference. Cab occupants: the man is the driver in the screen-left seat, both hands gripping the steering wheel, actively driving. The woman is the passenger in the screen-right seat; she only sits in the cab and never holds or reaches for the wheel — her hands stay on her lap, the dash, or the door. Two survivors in scavenged cold-weather gear. LOCATION MAP Wide frozen plateau. A snow-packed highway runs horizontally across the frame, entering from the deep-background left edge (x 5%, y 42%) and sweeping toward the right edge, so all travel reads clearly left-to-right; the road ahead (screen-right) is open and clear. Along the entire length of the road, from the far establishing distance onward: a scattered frozen convoy of abandoned, ice-encased trucks and cars — half-buried in drifted snow, some overturned on their roofs or sides, rusted and frost-covered, the wreckage of those who came before. Plus leaning ice ridges and packed-snow barricades, a collapsed highway sign sagging across the lane, dead ice-crusted utility poles with sagging wires. The 200-meter-tall wall of churning white blizzard/avalanche is always behind the truck — anchored to screen-left and the rear horizon, advancing rightward in pursuit but never ahead of the truck and never in its direction of travel. @monst lives inside this rear storm wall and rises out of it, always trailing behind the truck. Weak sun glow hidden behind the storm wall acts as backlight. Lighting direction: diffuse top-down with cold backlit haze from behind/left. FIRST FRAME AND SPATIAL BLOCKING The first visible frame is an extreme super-wide aerial-height establishing vista — maximum scope, @truck1 a tiny speck. The truck sits deep background, left-of-center, x 38%, y 44%, scale tiny (under 4% of frame width), driving left-to-right at full speed. Already visible in this wide establishing frame, scattered along the roadside and the snow plain, are abandoned snow-covered trucks and cars — some upright and half-buried, some overturned — dotting the route ahead and behind, establishing the frozen graveyard the truck will weave through. The blizzard wall fills the background behind and to the left, x 0% to 70%, upper half of frame, advancing rightward — always behind the truck, never in the open road ahead. No empty plate — the truck is present and moving left-to-right in frame one. The camera pushes in and tracks right with the truck until the cab and windshield dominate, x 50%, y 50%, the man driving (screen-left seat) and the woman passenger (screen-right seat) clearly visible through the glass. Later @monst surges out of the storm behind the truck. Truck travel direction: screen-left to screen-right, locked; clear road ahead at screen-right. Storm and monster locked to screen-left/rear (behind). Male driver: torso forward (screen-right), both hands gripping the steering wheel, eyes flicking to the side mirror back toward the storm (screen-left). Female passenger: torso forward, head snapping back toward @monst (screen-left) then front; hands brace on the dash or lap, never on the wheel. Both bodies face direction of travel (screen-right). FORMAT MODE Controlled six-segment sequence in one continuous escalating beat. Seg 1 real-time push-in. Seg 2 real-time obstacle gauntlet. Seg 3 real-time "shark-under-snow" stalk. Seg 4 slow-motion fake-out (claw miss). Seg 5 slow-motion scoop, launch, and mid-air rotations. Seg 6 low ground-level shot as the truck rolls in and stops upside down. HARD CUT at 3.0s, HARD CUT at 6.0s, SMASH CUT to slow motion at 9.0s, continue slow motion through the scoop, MATCH CUT to the ground angle at 13.5s. No subtitles, no music. OPTICS Prime-lens optical character throughout — clean, sharp center field, gentle natural falloff, no zoom-breathing, fixed-focal clarity. LENS LOCK SEG 1 = begin 107° wide rectilinear (immense environment, straight horizon, truck tiny, no fisheye), settling toward 84° classic wide as it closes on the cab. No drift beyond this push. LENS LOCK SEG 2 = 84° classic wide, low and close, foreground wrecks and barricades looming and ripping past the lens, immersive speed, straight lines rectilinear. LENS LOCK SEG 3 = 47° standard normal side profile, truck and the moving snow-bulge both readable, grounded perspective. LENS LOCK SEG 4 = 29° short telephoto, slight compression on the near-miss claw and the heroes' faces, the monster looming soft behind. LENS LOCK SEG 5 = 47° standard normal for the launch/flip — natural human-eye proportions, no distortion as the truck tumbles. LENS LOCK SEG 6 = 84° classic wide, low ground-level — the truck looms large rolling toward the lens, foreground snow road across the lower frame, no fisheye. CAMERA ARRI Alexa large-format cinema-camera character — wide tonal latitude, gentle highlight roll-off in the bright snow, clean shadow detail, true filmic color science. Seg 1: high aerial height over the plateau for max scope, then descends and pushes in while tracking right with @truck1, accelerating to match it, ending at windshield height ~2 m off the front-left quarter. Powerful weighted aerial-to-chase glide, subtle operator weight, no jitter, no drone float. Seg 2: HARD CUT to a low fast tracking shot riding just ahead of and beside the plow blade as the truck smashes and weaves through the wreck field — foreground debris whipping past, a brief duck under the collapsed sign. Seg 3: HARD CUT to a side tracking shot pacing the truck's flank; camera tilts back toward the rear to catch a fast snow-bulge wave coursing under the road surface behind the truck, then whips up as @monst bursts out of the snow behind it. Seg 4: SMASH CUT to slow motion — a tighter three-quarter rear angle on the cab as the monster's clawed hand lunges in from behind and rakes past, just missing; camera catches the heroes' faces. Seg 5: stay in slow motion, ease wider as the hand plunges into the ground behind/under the truck and heaves it up; camera holds the airborne truck and follows it through its rotations, debris and snow crossing the lens. Seg 6: MATCH CUT to a static low-angle ground shot — camera planted on the snow road, lens just above the surface, locked off. The truck drops in, lands, and rolls straight toward camera, growing huge, then settles upside down right in front of the lens. No camera move. ACTION TIMING 0.0s to 3.0s — Seg 1: Extreme super-wide establishing vista, tiny @truck1 flooring it left-to-right along a road dotted with abandoned, snow-covered and overturned trucks and cars, blizzard wall surging from screen-left behind. Continuous push-in and right-track resolves to a close-up of the two heroes — the man white-knuckle on the wheel, the woman glancing back. @monst's face surfaces from inside the churning wall behind, gaining. 3.0s HARD CUT. 3.0s to 6.0s — Seg 2: Obstacle gauntlet. The steel plow blade smashes through a packed-snow barricade in an explosion of powder, the truck swerves hard around the ice-encased hulk of a dead semi, clips a torus of ice debris, and ducks low under a collapsed highway sign. Snow spray, ice shards, real speed and near-misses. 6.0s HARD CUT. 6.0s to 9.0s — Seg 3: The shark stalk. @monst dives beneath the snow behind the truck and disappears; a long, fast-moving bulge-wave of churning snow races under the road surface behind the truck, gaining — the heroes spot it in the mirror, dread rising. The wave surges, then @monst erupts upward out of the snow in a tower of powder, hauling its towering body free of the storm directly behind @truck1. 9.0s SMASH CUT to slow motion. 9.0s to 11.0s — Seg 4: The fake-out. In slow motion @monst lunges from behind and swings its huge clawed hand at the truck — the claws rake through the air and just miss the cargo box by inches, tearing only snow and a side mirror. For a held beat the heroes' faces flood with split-second relief, believing they've broken free. 11.0s to 13.5s — Seg 5: The reversal. Still in slow motion, the monster's hand drops and plunges into the ground behind and under the truck, scooping up a wave of frozen earth and snow that levers @truck1 into the air. The truck tumbles through two full mid-air rotations, glass and snow spraying outward, the heroes' relief snapping back to terror. 13.5s MATCH CUT to the low ground-level angle. 13.5s to 16.0s — Seg 6: Static low ground-level shot. The truck drops into frame, hits the snow road and rolls toward camera in a spray of powder, decelerating, and comes to rest upside down right in front of the lens, filling the frame. One front wheel keeps spinning freely for a beat, then slows and stops. Loose snow drifts down, steam and dust settle. PERFORMANCE (Hollywood-grade acting) Naturalistic, restrained, high-stakes performances captured like seasoned A-list film actors, with visible tension and real fear riding under the control. The male driver: jaw clenched, brow tight, a hard swallow, eyes wide and darting between the road, the mirror, and the swerving wrecks, white-knuckle grip as he muscles the wheel — fear held back by focus. The woman passenger: breath catching, eyes wide tracking the snow-bulge and the monster behind, a flinch, a hand braced hard on the dash; she never touches the wheel. On the fake-out, both faces flash a half-second of desperate relief — a gasp, the smallest easing — instantly ripped back into raw terror as the truck is launched, heads and limbs thrown against belts. Genuine adrenaline and dread throughout, no stiff or doll-like faces, no exaggerated mugging. Two survivors who have done this before, scared but capable. PHYSICS Driving on snow: @truck1 rides on purpose-built winter rubber — massive deep-lug snow tires with aggressive blocky tread, sipes and metal studs biting into the packed snow, plus studded rear snow-tracks for flotation. Tires visibly compress and grip, deforming against the snow, finding traction and occasionally breaking loose into brief controlled slip; tread packs and sheds snow each rotation. The heavy chassis sits low and planted, suspension compressing over ridges and lurching through swerves, throwing continuous white snow-spray and powder rooster-tails. Exhaust stacks vent hot steam shearing backward (screen-left). Engine at full strain, genuinely heavy mass and momentum. Obstacles: the plow blade meets each barricade with real impact — packed snow shatters and sprays, ice slabs crack and tumble with weight, the truck's mass barely deflects as it punches through; swerves load the suspension and shift the chassis with believable inertia and grip-loss. Shark stalk: @monst submerged under the snow displaces a raised, fast-moving bulge of churning powder that plows forward like a wake behind the truck, snow cascading off its crest; on the burst-out, a column of snow erupts upward with real mass and gravity as the monster heaves free, lower body still trailing back into the churning storm. Fake-out: the clawed hand carries real mass and speed as it swipes through the air just behind the truck, shredding a curtain of snow and clipping the side mirror, missing the body — believable near-contact, wind and powder blasting off the truck in its wake. Launch and crash: the scooping hand levers @truck1 upward by an erupting wave of frozen earth and snow. It leaves the ground carrying its full mass and load — heavy engine block, armor plating, fuel weight pulling the center of gravity low and forward — so it rotates with believable, slightly sluggish angular momentum, not a light flip. During the airborne rotations every wheel and track stays firmly mounted in its correct factory position — wheels keep spinning under their own inertia but never detach, dislodge, shift along the axle, droop, or float out of place; front tires stay on the front axle, rear tracks stay at the rear, all geometry intact. Panels and glass deform and shatter in slow arcs, loose gear and debris fly with correct mass and gravity. On landing, the heaviest mass (engine/front) makes first hard contact, the roof and cab crumple under the full weight as the chassis slams down and rolls toward camera with real momentum, decelerating against the snow's friction, settling upside down with real weight and load distribution. The wheels remain mounted; one front wheel keeps spinning freely on its axle, then slows under friction and stops. No floaty, weightless, or rubbery motion. Storm: blizzard wall churns with turbulent rolling motion, particles driven forward by wind, denser at the base, always closing on the truck from behind. @monst: a towering form of compacted swirling snow and ice — jagged ice teeth, hollow pale-glowing eye-sockets, frost-crystal features, a massive hand of fused ice shards that plunges into the ground and heaves it upward. It coalesces and partly dissolves, emerging from the rear storm and never fully solid, moving with the storm; its scoop carries crushing earth-moving force. Bodies: the driver's arms carry the steering wheel's weight; the woman's hair and scarf lag and whip; through the launch and flips both bodies obey real inertia. COLOR GRADE AND ATMOSPHERE Match the reference frame: a cold teal-and-cyan cinematic grade, heavily desaturated, low saturation across the board with everything pushed toward steel-blue and blue-green. Crushed, slightly lifted blacks holding milky shadow detail in the haze, soft filmic highlight roll-off, gentle low contrast from dense atmosphere. Thick volumetric blizzard haze and drifting snow particles fill the depth, softening every edge and stacking misty layers between camera and background so @monst reads as a ghostly, half-dissolved silhouette of textured ice rising out of the rear storm, with two pale, softly glowing almond eyes and rows of translucent icicle teeth looming over the scene. Light wraps and diffuses through the snow-fog. The truck's small practical lamps — amber/orange running lights and warm headlights — are the only warm accents in the frame, glowing as muted pops against the overwhelming cold blue palette, slightly bloomed through the haze. Overall mood: ominous, monumental, frostbitten, painterly cold — Roger Deakins-style atmospheric teal twilight. LIGHTING Cold overcast daylight, flat and diffuse, exposed for the bright snow field with Alexa-style highlight roll-off so the snow holds detail. Hidden sun glow behind the rear storm wall backlights the blizzard so @monst reads as a brighter churning silhouette against the gloom. Truck surfaces catch cool top light with hard cold speculars on wet metal and ice; cab interior falls into cooler shadow, faces revealed by soft windshield bounce, dash glow, and the spill of the warm practical lamps. Cold blue-cyan key everywhere; the only warm light is the truck's amber running lights and headlights as small contained accents — no broad warm key, no studio fill. WARDROBE (Mad Max, snow-logical) Male driver: scavenged cold-weather warlord gear — fur-lined battered leather greatcoat over a quilted thermal parka, riveted metal pauldron on one shoulder, fingerless gloves over thermal liners, frost-fogged ski goggles pushed up on a fur-flap ushanka cap, respirator scarf at the neck, bone and scrap-metal ornaments. Weathered, ice-crusted, lived-in. Female passenger: hooded fur-collared shearling jacket cinched with leather harness straps and buckles, knitted balaclava pulled down, goggles around the neck, fingerless gloves, scavenged-metal arm guards. Practical, insulated, battle-worn — same scrap-and-fur aesthetic, never impractical for sub-zero cold. STYLE Large-scale fantasy-realism in the vein of George Miller's kinetic wasteland chase staging, Denis Villeneuve's monumental scale and cold restraint, Peter Jackson's tactile creature physicality, and Zack Snyder's heroic backlit silhouettes and slow-motion impact beats. Photoreal texture, real grain, true film color science, grounded physical cinema — epic scope that still feels captured on a real set. 8K photorealistic, anamorphic widescreen, epic dark-fantasy cold blockbuster grade (Peter Jackson / Villeneuve weight), fine grain, hyperdetailed. OUTPUT SETTINGS IMAX-grade 8K capture, high bitrate 80–120 Mbps, ARRI Alexa large-format sensor character, prime-lens rendering, anamorphic widescreen, real-time motion in Segments 1–3, slow motion in Segments 4–5, ground-level static in Segment 6. POSITIVE LOCKS On the opening super-wide establishing shot, abandoned snow-covered trucks and cars (some overturned) are already visible scattered along the road and plain, making the later wreck-gauntlet logical and consistent. The man is the driver in the screen-left seat with both hands on the steering wheel, driving the whole shot. The woman is the passenger in the screen-right seat; she only sits and never holds, grabs, or reaches for the steering wheel. The snow blizzard/avalanche is ALWAYS behind the truck, chasing it — it never appears ahead of the truck or in its direction of travel; the road ahead (screen-right) stays clear. @monst is always behind the truck and always appears out of the storm — it emerges from the blizzard, submerges beneath the snow like a shark and travels as a moving snow-bulge wave behind the truck before bursting out, never a separate creature on clear open ground and never ahead of the truck. The chase passes through a real obstacle gauntlet — the plow blade smashes through ice barricades, the truck weaves around a frozen convoy of wrecks and ducks under a collapsed sign. Before the finale there is a fake-out: the monster's clawed hand lunges from behind and just misses the truck, the heroes feel a half-second of relief, then the hand digs under the truck and heaves the ground upward, launching it. The monster does not destroy the truck with claws; the flip comes from the under-scoop. The launch and two mid-air rotations play in slow motion with the truck's real mass and load — heavy, weighted, never floaty. During the entire airborne flip the wheels and tracks stay fixed in their correct mounted positions, spinning but never detaching or shifting. The final beat is a static low-angle ground shot with the camera on the snow road: the truck rolls in toward the lens and stops upside down right in front of camera, one front wheel still spinning before it slows and stops. @truck1 runs on special deep-lug studded snow tires and snow-tracks, travels left-to-right, and the shot opens on an extreme super-wide establishing vista before pushing in to the heroes. Cold teal/cyan desaturated grade with thick blizzard haze; @monst reads as a ghostly ice silhouette with pale glowing eyes; the truck's amber lamps are the only warm accents. Faces carry visible tension and fear; performances stay photoreal and restrained, no stiff or artificial faces.
~~~~

### cinematic film scene #87 — Scene 5 - The truck vs the snow monster

- Source: https://higgsfield.ai/blog/seedance4k-breakdown ([notes](seedance4k-breakdown.md))
- Model / settings: Assets: GPT Image 2 (4K sheets), Nano Banana Pro (props), Soul Cinema (locations/characters); video: Seedance 2.0 4K; prompts written by a Claude "prompt-writer" skill

~~~~text
SCENE CONTEXT A lived-in living room toward evening. A man sits on the front edge of the sofa facing a flatscreen TV across the room, leaning forward toward the screen with a remote loosely in hand, watching a video playing on the TV. The camera is anchored behind and slightly to one side of the sofa, looking over his lowered head toward the fully visible screen. One continuous static 6-second shot.ACTIVE REFERENCES @ADIL — the man, seen from behind and three-quarter back: leaning slightly forward, perched on the front edge of the sofa, off the backrest, back and shoulders and back of head to camera, low and to one side so he never blocks the TV. Same wardrobe throughout. 100% matches the reference. @REMOTE — the remote: normal household size, held loosely in his hand, pointed loosely toward the TV but not pressed. 100% matches the reference. @ADIL'S-ROOM — STYLE REFERENCE ONLY, not a fixed keyframe: the same lived-in living room, sofa facing the flatscreen across the room, soft daylight fading through the side window, faint haze in the air. Extend the world naturally; do not reproduce the reference frame 1:1. @video_1 — the footage playing on the TV: on-screen content matches the source 1:1 — same image, timing, framing, motion and colors, never reinterpreted, re-edited, cropped, looped early, or replaced.LOCATION MAP Foreground (low, frame-left): the back of the man's head and shoulders, out of focus, rim-lit by the TV; @REMOTE the remote in his hand. Midground: the sofa front edge and the floor between sofa and TV. Background (center, sharp): the flatscreen on the far wall showing @video_1, keeping the exact same screen size and position it has in the @ADIL'S-ROOM room; the side window frame-left spilling fading daylight. Camera anchored behind the sofa on the shadow side. Warm room neutrals dominate, cool TV-screen glow as the secondary tone, a chartreuse #d1ef17 accent on the remote standby LED and a glint of screen glow.FIRST FRAME / BLOCKING From frame one the TV is already playing @video_1 and is fully visible. The man is already seated on the front edge, leaning forward toward the screen, remote in hand, head low in the foreground, breathing and faintly shifting — nothing is frozen. Rule of thirds: head and shoulders sit low and to one side of the foreground so the flatscreen stays fully visible and unobstructed center-frame.FORMAT MODE One continuous shot, the camera does not cut and does not move on its own.OPTICS Single static medium-wide at 47° FOV, framing the man's head and shoulders in the lower foreground and the whole flatscreen unobstructed in the background. Real cine-lens character, 180° shutter motion blur. No drift.CAMERA Locked off behind the sofa on the shadow side, eye-level-low, slightly off the subject's axis so his head sits low and to one side and never overlaps the TV, and so the screen is seen at a slight off-axis angle. Focus held on the TV screen, the foreground head soft. No pan, no push, no shake. Wide tonal latitude, soft highlight roll-off holding the bright screen.ACTION 0.0s to 6.0s — @video_1 plays on the TV continuously and unchanged, matching the source 1:1. The man sits leaning forward toward the screen, perched on the front edge of the sofa, off the backrest, the remote @REMOTE resting in his hand, watching. He breathes, his shoulders settle and shift a little, the back of his head and hair catch the cool shifting glow of the screen. The camera stays fully static the entire time.PERFORMANCE Top-tier cinematic restraint — small live shifts of the shoulders and head, slow natural breathing, a deliberate human stillness while watching. Pore-level realism: vellus hair, asymmetric moles, capillary flush, pore-shadows matching the window and screen light, rim glow on the hair from the TV.PHYSICS Real body weight on the front edge of the cushions, the cushions compressing where he sits, clear contact shadows under him on the seat. The remote rests in his hand at normal household size with real weight. No floating props.LIGHTING Window key from frame-left, warm and fading toward evening, dimmer as it goes. Cool flatscreen glow as fill, its hue shifting with the content of @video_1, rimming the back of his hair and shoulders. The screen surface catches a soft reflection of the room and window. White balance fixed at 5200K. Light atmospheric haze softening the depth between camera and TV.SCREEN REALISM The TV picture reads as a real physical panel being filmed, not a clean digital overlay: the picture sits behind the glass panel and carries a faint glass sheen and soft glare, dim reflections of the room and window on the screen surface, a subtle pixel/sub-pixel grid and gentle moiré, slight bloom on the brightest areas, mild panel contrast and color shift, and a small off-axis perspective from the side viewing angle. The underlying picture stays fully recognizable as @video_1.AUDIO NO MUSIC. SFX ONLY — diegetic sound and live audio throughout. No score, no soundtrack, no background music of any kind. The native audio of @video_1 from the TV, plus quiet room tone. No subtitles, no added voiceover.STYLE 8K photorealistic live-action cinematic, real lens capture, naturalistic master cinematography. 24fps, real-time, smooth motion, no jitter, 16:9, total duration 6 seconds.POSITIVE LOCKS The TV picture matches @video_1 1:1 in content, timing and color, fully recognizable throughout — while looking like a real filmed panel with glass sheen, soft glare, faint room reflections, a subtle pixel grid and gentle moiré, mild bloom and a slight off-axis angle. The flatscreen stays fully visible and unobstructed — the man's head stays low and to one side. He leans forward toward the TV and stays off the backrest the entire time, seated on the front edge of the sofa. The remote @REMOTE stays resting in his hand, normal household size, not pressed. The camera stays fully static and locked off, one continuous shot, no cut, no move. The flatscreen keeps the exact same size and position as in the @ADIL'S-ROOM room the entire time — it does not grow, shrink, or move. Screen content stays unbranded — no logos or UI beyond what is already in @video_1. Same @ADIL identity and wardrobe throughout. Contact shadows under him read clearly. No eye glow.
~~~~

### cinematic film scene #88 — Scene 6 - The close-up on the remote

~~~~text
SCENE CONTEXT Extreme close-up of @ADIL's left hand resting on his left thigh, holding @REMOTE, his thumb making one single deliberate channel-up press. The lived-in living room falls into soft blur behind. One continuous static close-up, 4 seconds.ACTIVE REFERENCES @REMOTE — matte black household streaming remote. 100% matches the reference. Face layout top to bottom: chartreuse "higgs 4K" logo and a round power button at top; a round navigation pad with up/down/left/right chevrons below; then a lower row of two rockers — a VOL rocker on the LEFT ("+" top, "VOL" mid, "−" bottom) and a CH rocker on the RIGHT (UP chevron ˄ top, "CH" mid, DOWN chevron ˅ bottom). The reference's red arrow points precisely to the CH-UP chevron (˄) directly above the "CH" label. @ADIL'S-ROOM — STYLE REFERENCE ONLY, not a fixed keyframe; the out-of-focus backdrop carries this room's character. Model extends the world, never reproduced 1:1.LOCATION MAP Foreground (sharp, on the strong third): the left hand and @REMOTE on the thigh, the CH rocker and thumb. Midground: the thigh and lap falling off into softness. Background: the room reduced to a soft out-of-focus wash of warm neutral surfaces and furniture silhouettes from @ADIL'S-ROOM. Window light enters from frame-left; a cool spill comes from off-frame front. Camera anchored on the shadow side.FIRST FRAME / BLOCKING Non-empty opening frame: the left hand already resting on the left thigh holding @REMOTE, thumb resting just over the CH-UP chevron, alive and steady — nothing frozen, faint breathing motion. Rule of thirds: the remote runs on a slight diagonal, CH rocker and thumb on the strong third, tight on hand and remote with the soft room wash behind.FORMAT MODE One continuous static close-up; the camera does not cut and does not move on its own. 4-second clock, one single press only.OPTICS Extreme close-up at 12° FOV, tight on the lower-right of @REMOTE and the thumb — hand and remote crisp, background dissolving into soft out-of-focus blur. Shallow depth of field, prime tele-detail character, 180° shutter motion blur. No drift.CAMERA Locked off on the shadow side, eye-level to the remote, held just above and in front of the hand looking down at the remote face on the thigh, CH rocker and thumb centered. Focus held on the CH-UP chevron and the thumb. No pan, no push, no shake. Wide tonal latitude, soft highlight roll-off on the matte plastic and skin.ACTION 0.0s–1.0s — the left hand rests on the left thigh holding @REMOTE, thumb resting on the lower-right face just over the CH-UP chevron (˄) directly above the "CH" label, where the red arrow points; matte black surface catching soft cool ambient light. 1.0s–2.0s — the thumb presses the CH-UP chevron (˄) one single time — a deliberate press, the button giving slightly under the pad, one soft mechanical click as the channel changes. The thumb stays clear of the VOL rocker to the left, the navigation pad above, and the CH-DOWN chevron (˅) below — only CH-UP. 2.0s–4.0s — the thumb lifts off and settles back, the hand resting steady on the thigh, @REMOTE held still, no further presses.PERFORMANCE One deliberate, unhurried thumb press — settles on the button, presses down once with a small give, releases. Pore-level realism on the hand: vellus hair, knuckle creases, nail detail, capillary flush, pore-shadows matching the soft window and ambient light.PHYSICS @REMOTE rests with real weight in the grip on the thigh; the thumb pad compresses the CH-UP chevron once with a tiny travel and release; the hand stays steady through the press. No floating props.LIGHTING Soft window daylight from frame-left as warm key, fading toward evening; a cool off-frame-front spill as fill, rimming the knuckles and the top edge of the remote. Faint atmospheric haze. WB locked 5200K. The remote stays plain matte plastic lit only by ambient light — surface and buttons unlit, no glow anywhere.COLOR GRADE Warm room neutrals on skin and matte black plastic as the dominant material, the cool off-frame spill as the secondary beam catching the knuckles and remote edge, and a chartreuse #d1ef17 accent living only on the "higgs 4K" logo — color tied to surface and light, not a flat list.AUDIO NO MUSIC. SFX ONLY — diegetic sound and live audio throughout. No score, no soundtrack, no background music of any kind. One single soft mechanical button click on the press, quiet room tone.STYLE 8K photorealistic live-action macro, naturalistic real-lens capture, fine grain. 24fps, real-time, smooth motion.OUTPUT SETTINGS 16:9. Real-time, total duration 4 seconds.POSITIVE LOCKS It is the LEFT hand on the LEFT thigh holding @REMOTE, the backdrop a soft out-of-focus wash of @ADIL'S-ROOM. The thumb presses the CH-UP chevron (˄) directly above "CH" on the lower-right CH rocker, where the reference red arrow points — exactly ONE time, a single clear press-and-release, then it lifts off and the hand holds still. @REMOTE stays matte black with the chartreuse "higgs 4K" logo, lit only by ambient light, surface and buttons unlit with no glow. Framing stays tight on the hand and CH rocker. @REMOTE stays normal household size. Static locked-off close-up, 4 seconds, real-time, no cut, no move. Eyes/skin natural — no glow.
~~~~

### cinematic film scene #90 — Scene 7 - Flipping the channels

- Source: https://higgsfield.ai/blog/seedance4k-breakdown ([notes](seedance4k-breakdown.md))
- Model / settings: Assets: GPT Image 2 (4K sheets), Nano Banana Pro (props), Soul Cinema (locations/characters); video: Seedance 2.0 4K; prompts written by a Claude "prompt-writer" skill

~~~~text
SCENE CONTEXT Lived-in living room toward evening. @ADIL sits leaning forward on the front edge of the sofa, watching a small flatscreen across the room, @REMOTE in his left hand. He flips channels — news, two ads, then a sweeping fantasy film — and reacts. The shot is always the living room: a small ordinary TV across the room plays everything as a little inset picture. Two shots, one hard cut, 10-second clock.ACTIVE REFERENCES @ADIL — the man. 100% matches the reference. Shot 1: from behind, leaning forward on the front edge, back/shoulders/back of head to camera, face turned away, low and to one side so the small TV stays fully visible, @REMOTE in his LEFT hand. Shot 2: face revealed in a medium reaction, LEFT arm extended with @REMOTE pointed at the TV, then lowering it. Same wardrobe throughout. @REMOTE — normal household TV remote in his LEFT hand, chartreuse standby LED, pointed at the TV through the presses. 100% matches the reference. @ADIL'S-ROOM — STYLE REFERENCE ONLY, not a fixed keyframe; model extends the world freely, never reproduced 1:1. @IMAGE4 — the fantasy film shown inside the little TV: a white, gold-spired castle on a misty grey mountain ridge above a vast green meadow at golden sunrise, drifting mist. 100% matches the reference, displayed only inside the small TV rectangle.LOCATION MAP The frame is always the living room, camera behind the sofa. Foreground (low, frame-left): the back of @ADIL's head and shoulders, full-size, rim-lit by the screen, @REMOTE in his left hand. Midground: the sofa front edge and the floor across the room. Background (upper-middle, ~3–4 m away): a small ordinary flatscreen TV filling roughly a quarter (~25%) of the frame, framed by its black bezel and the wall around it; the side window frame-left spilling fading daylight. Everything that plays stays a small picture inside that little TV rectangle — the room and the back of his head stay full-size around it. Camera on the shadow side. Shot 2 re-sets in front on his face.FIRST FRAME / BLOCKING Non-empty opening frame: the little distant TV already playing the news, @ADIL already seated leaning forward, head low in the foreground, breathing and faintly shifting — nothing frozen. Rule of thirds: his head sits low and to one side so the small flatscreen stays fully visible in the upper-middle background. The whole room is in view.FORMAT MODE Timed multishot, exactly ONE HARD CUT at 0:07; the camera otherwise holds still behind the sofa with the whole room in view. 10-second clock.OPTICS SHOT 1 (0:00–0:07): medium-wide at 47° FOV, @ADIL's head and shoulders full-size low in the foreground, the small flatscreen (~25% of frame) unobstructed in the upper-middle background. SHOT 2 (0:07–0:10): medium at 29° FOV, eye-level on his face, his extended LEFT arm and @REMOTE in frame. Real cine-lens character, 180° shutter motion blur. No drift mid-segment.CAMERA SHOT 1: fully static, locked off, positioned BEHIND the sofa on the shadow side, looking over @ADIL's lowered head across the room toward the small distant flatscreen, the whole room held in view, his face turned away — no move. SHOT 2: static, locked off, re-set in front at eye level on his face, the extended arm and remote in frame — no move. Wide tonal latitude, soft highlight roll-off holding the bright little screen.ACTION 0:00–0:02 — SHOT 1: on the small distant flatscreen the news plays — a single anchor at a clean studio desk speaking straight to camera, saying in English "Seedance 4K is unreal." @ADIL sits leaning forward, watching, cool screen glow rimming his hair; the room fills the frame, the TV stays small in the background. 0:02–0:04 — still SHOT 1: he raises his LEFT hand with @REMOTE and presses — soft click — the little screen changes to Advertisement 1: a clear faceted glass perfume bottle of golden liquid slowly rotating on a mirrored surface, light refracting into soft rainbow glints, a gentle puff of mist; the room glow turns warm gold. 0:04–0:06 — he presses again — soft click — the little screen changes to Advertisement 2: a sleek silver sports car with a smooth seamless polished body gliding along a coastal cliff road at golden hour, sea glittering below, a thin dust trail behind the tyres; the glow shifts cool blue. 0:06–0:07 — he presses a third time — soft click — the little screen changes to @IMAGE4: the castle on the misty ridge above the green meadow at sunrise, staying small inside the distant TV, warm golden glow washing the room. The room and his head stay full-size around the small TV. 0:07 HARD CUT. 0:07–0:10 — SHOT 2: medium on his face, eye-level, his LEFT arm still extended with @REMOTE pointed at the TV. A micro-pause, then moderate surprise — eyebrows lift, eyes widen a touch, a small open-mouth beat — and he says "WOW," lowering his LEFT arm on the same beat as the word. Restrained and human.PERFORMANCE Shot 1 (from behind): deliberate, unhurried raise of the arm and presses, body settled on the front edge. Shot 2: a held micro-pause before the reaction, eyebrows lifting, eyes widening a touch, lips parting for a single "Wow" — moderate, genuine surprise, never cartoonish. Pore-level realism: vellus hair, asymmetric moles, capillary flush, wet living eyes with screen catch-lights, visible breath and chest rise.PHYSICS Real body weight on the front edge of the cushions, the cushions compressing where he sits, clear contact shadows under him on the seat. @REMOTE carries real weight in his left hand; each press gives a tiny travel and release. No floating props.LIGHTING Window key from frame-left, warm and fading toward evening, dimmer as it goes; camera on the shadow side; faint atmospheric haze. Cool flatscreen glow as fill, its hue shifting with each channel — warm gold on the perfume, cool blue on the car, warm gold on @IMAGE4 — rimming the back of his hair and shoulders. In Shot 2 the screen glow lights his face from frame-front. WB locked 5200K.COLOR GRADE Warm fading-daylight room neutrals as the dominant material, the cool TV-screen glow as the secondary beam (shifting hue per channel), and a chartreuse #d1ef17 accent living only on the @REMOTE standby LED and a flicker of the screen glow — color tied to source and surface, not a flat list.AUDIO NO MUSIC. SFX ONLY — diegetic sound and live audio throughout. No score, no soundtrack, no background music of any kind. News anchor (from the TV, English): "Seedance 4K is unreal." Then a few soft channel-change clicks with abrupt TV-audio shifts; soft ambient wind atmosphere as @IMAGE4 comes up. @ADIL (Shot 2, English): "Wow." Room tone. No subtitles.STYLE 8K photorealistic live-action — no 3D render, no game engine, no game-cutscene aesthetic. Naturalistic master cinematography, fine film grain.OUTPUT SETTINGS 16:9. Real-time throughout. Total duration 10 seconds.POSITIVE LOCKS The frame is always the living room, camera behind the sofa with the whole room in view. The TV is a small ordinary television filling about a quarter of the frame in the upper-middle background; every channel — news, both ads, and @IMAGE4 — appears as a small picture inside the distant TV's bezel, the room and the back of @ADIL's head full-size around it. Exactly one HARD CUT, at 0:07; otherwise the camera holds still. @ADIL leans forward on the front edge and uses his LEFT hand with @REMOTE pointed at the TV through three unhurried switches: news → perfume ad → sports-car ad → @IMAGE4. His head stays low and to one side so the small TV stays fully visible. Shot 2 reveals his face for the "WOW" with his LEFT arm extended, then he lowers it on the same beat as the word — moderate, natural, restrained surprise. Same @ADIL identity and wardrobe across the cut. TV and @REMOTE stay normal household size. Contact shadows under him on the sofa read clearly. Eyes stay natural — no eye glow.
~~~~

### cinematic film scene #91 — Scene 8 - The epic battle

- Source: https://higgsfield.ai/blog/seedance4k-breakdown ([notes](seedance4k-breakdown.md))
- Model / settings: Assets: GPT Image 2 (4K sheets), Nano Banana Pro (props), Soul Cinema (locations/characters); video: Seedance 2.0 4K; prompts written by a Claude "prompt-writer" skill

~~~~text
SCENE CONTEXT One continuous, fast and dynamic FPV-drone battle shot with a dramatic scale shift. An elven army charges in from the LEFT with swords; an orc horde charges to meet it from the RIGHT, every clash pairing an elf against an orc. The drone flies fast through the middle of the clash, weaving between the fighting bodies, then dives down between TALL blades of grass to ground level — where two tiny, palm-sized fighters duel one-on-one while the army battle rages above.ACTIVE REFERENCES @castle — green grass field rising to the white-and-gold spired elven citadel on a misty grey mountain, low dawn sun flaring screen-right, soft mist drifting down the slopes. 100% matches the reference (STYLE REFERENCE ONLY, model extends the world). @elf — STYLE reference for the elven army: silver-and-gold leaf-filigree armor, pale-blue capes, elven swords. The army is a varied host — men and women, each a distinct face, hair color, build, helmet and cape, every elf unique while keeping this armor-and-weapon style, each fighting with a sword and striking only at orcs. 100% matches the reference style. @orc — the army orcs: dry, upright SOLDIER orcs in battered black-iron plate, green-grey skin, tusks, amber eyes, heavy iron weapons — ordinary orc-sized, lean and brutish, about a head taller than the elves, each unique, striking only at elves, staying in the large army up top. 100% matches the reference. @flower-warrior — forest knight: carved wooden face with glowing amber eyes, orange flower-petal mane, green leaf mantle, bark skin, vine-wrapped spear and a glowing amber-cored shield. 100% matches the reference. @troll — BOG TROLL: a hulking, hunched swamp troll, thick green hide caked in wet moss, slick algae and dripping mud, long trailing swamp-weed hair, big jutting tusks, small amber eyes, ragged hide-and-scrap armor and a rope harness, gripping a heavy pitted rusty sword. A wet, mossy, primitive swamp creature — a troll, not an orc, clearly different from the dry black-iron soldiers above. 100% matches the reference.LOCATION MAP The world of @castle. Background: the white-gold spired citadel on the misty grey mountain, dawn sun flaring screen-right, mist down the slopes, bright battle bokeh up high. Midground: the two charging lines colliding across the open grass field — elves from screen-LEFT, orcs from screen-RIGHT. Foreground (ground beat): TALL wet grass blades, soil, dew, with the normal-scale soldiers' legs, feet and boots towering and crashing down like giants around the two palm-sized fighters. The drone path runs side-on along the lines → through the clash → down between the grass to the soil.SCALE LOCK Orcs and elves are ordinary human-scale warriors. @flower-warrior and @troll are TINY — each about the size of a human palm, standing as tall as a single blade of grass on the soil. Because they are so small, the normal-sized elves and orcs rise above them like giants, so at ground level the soldiers' legs, feet and boots fill the frame and crash down beside the two tiny fighters.FIRST FRAME / BLOCKING Non-empty opening frame: a wide SIDE view, both armies already charging and closing — elven swordsmen rushing in from screen-LEFT, orcs from screen-RIGHT, the two lines about to collide across @castle, citadel and screen-right sun behind. Everything already in motion, nothing frozen.FORMAT MODE One unbroken continuous take; a fast, free-flying FPV-drone camera holds the whole shot, the camera does not cut on its own.OPTICS Wide ~84° up high for scale and speed, tightening to ~47° as it weaves through the fighters, then macro ~18° at ground level among the grass. Deep focus up high, shallow macro focus down low. Strong motion blur on the fast moves. No drift mid-beat.CAMERA Aggressive, weightless, dynamic FPV-drone movement, always moving. Beat 1 — open on the wide side view: the two charging lines rushing together across @castle. Beat 2 — the drone flies fast and low THROUGH the middle of the clash, weaving and banking between the colliding elves and orcs, slipping past swinging swords. Beat 3 — it whips downward and punches between the TALL grass blades to the soil, threading dynamically around the two palm-sized fighters while overhead the legs and boots of the normal-sized soldiers stomp and crash around the grass. Continuous, fluid, energetic.ACTION Up top the armies collide — elf against orc all across the line: each elven swordsman duels an orc, each orc swings at an elf, same-side allies fighting shoulder to shoulder against the enemy. As the drone reaches the grass, the two tiny fighters duel ONE-ON-ONE, fast and dynamic: @flower-warrior lunging and thrusting the vine spear, @troll parrying and counter-swinging the heavy rusty sword — both dodging, spinning, blades clashing, leaves and embers scattering, mud and water flicking off the troll, while orc and elf feet pound the earth nearby, just missing them, dust and dew bursting with each footfall.PERFORMANCE Up top: ferocious committed combat, varied unique faces straining, war-cry mouths, weighted swings. At ground level: @flower-warrior moves fast and agile, light on its feet; @troll moves with heavy lumbering mass, shoulders rolling, a slow brute power against the knight's speed — a clear contrast of styles, both reading as alive and reactive.PHYSICS Realistic momentum and weight — soldier footfalls send tremors and burst dust and dew at macro scale; the tall grass whips and bends in the drone's wake; @flower-warrior is fast and agile, @troll heavy and lumbering with water and mud dripping and flicking from its hide, both with weighty creak and squelch. Every pose flows continuously into the next, motion fully connected, no floaty movement.LIGHTING Cinematic dawn — warm low backlight and sun flare screen-right from @castle, soft mountain mist, volumetric light and atmospheric haze up high with bright battle bokeh. At ground level sun glints through the tall wet grass; the soldiers' legs cast moving shadows over the tiny fighters; @flower-warrior's amber core-glow and the wet sheen on @troll's mossy hide read against the shadowed soil.COLOR GRADE Warm dawn gold from the low backlit sun as the dominant beam on the grass and armor, cool misty grey-blue mountain atmosphere as the secondary tone, with accents of pale-blue elven capes, silver-gold filigree, the orange petal mane, amber shield-glow and the wet green moss of the troll — color tied to material and the dawn light, glowing and filmic, not a flat list.STYLE 8K photoreal cinematic, ultra-detailed, high-budget epic fantasy film look — natural skin, bark, hide, moss, metal and leaf textures with fine micro-detail, macro-photography texture on grass and soil, real organic grain, filmic grade.OUTPUT SETTINGS 16:9. Real-time, one continuous take.AUDIO NO MUSIC. SFX ONLY — diegetic sound and live audio throughout. No score, no soundtrack, no background music of any kind. Roaring charge, war cries, clashing steel and sword rings, thudding footsteps overhead; up close the fast sharp clashes of the two tiny weapons, wet squelching, dripping water, creaking wood, scattering leaves, bursting dew, wind through tall grass.POSITIVE LOCKS One continuous fast FPV-drone take — opens with elves charging from screen-LEFT and orcs from screen-RIGHT, flies dynamically THROUGH the battle, then dives down between TALL grass blades to ground level. Orcs and elves stay ordinary human-scale warriors. Every clash is elf versus orc — elves strike at orcs, orcs strike at elves, same-side allies fight side by side against the enemy line. The elven army follows the @elf style as a varied host — men and women, each a distinct face, hair and armor — every elf fighting with a sword in hand. At ground level there are exactly TWO tiny fighters: @flower-warrior versus @troll. The big army above is dry black-iron orc soldiers (@orc); the tiny ground fighter @troll is the moss-and-mud bog troll with a rusty sword — a separate creature, a troll and not an orc. Each orc and elf unique. Dynamic motion throughout, crisp stable readable picture, temporally consistent frames.
~~~~
